                            {% endfor %}
                        </tbody>
                    </table>
                </div>
                <div class="mt-3">
                    <a href="{% url 'hod_dashboard' %}" class="btn btn-secondary">
                        <i class="fas fa-arrow-left"></i> Back to Dashboard
                    </a>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>

    <script>
        (function() {
            const form = document.getElementById('filterForm');
            const searchInput = document.getElementById('searchInput');
            const designationSelect = document.getElementById('designationSelect');
            let timer = null;

            function submitForm() { form.submit(); }

            // Auto-submit on search with debounce
            searchInput.addEventListener('input', function() {
                clearTimeout(timer);
                timer = setTimeout(submitForm, 300);
            });

            searchInput.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {
                if (e.key === 'Enter') {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    clearTimeout(timer);
                    submitForm();
                }
            });

            // Auto-submit on designation change
            designationSelect.addEventListener('change', submitForm);
        })();
    </script>
    <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/bootstrap@5.1.3/dist/js/bootstrap.bundle.min.js"></script>
</body>
</html>

                                    <td>{% if rec.face_verified %}<i class="fas fa-check-circle text-success"></i>{% else %}<i class="fas fa-times-circle text-danger"></i>{% endif %}</td>

                                </tr>
                                {% empty %}
                                <tr><td colspan="6" class="text-center py-4 text-muted">No records found.</td></tr>
                                {% endfor %}
                            {% endif %}
                        </tbody>
                    </table>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

[74/91] 📁 templates/attendance/lecturer_mark_attendance.html
📏 Size: 11 KB | 🕐 Modified: 2026-07-28 12:13:49

<!-- templates/attendance/lecturer_mark_attendance.html -->
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
    <title>Mark Attendance - Lecturer</title>
    <link href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/bootstrap@5.1.3/dist/css/bootstrap.min.css" rel="stylesheet">
    <link href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/font-awesome/6.0.0/css/all.min.css" rel="stylesheet">
    <style>
        body { background: #f5f5f5; padding: 20px; }
        .navbar { background: linear-gradient(135deg, #667eea 0%, #764ba2 100%); border-radius: 12px; margin-bottom: 30px; }
        .navbar-brand, .nav-link { color: #fff !important; }
        .card { border-radius: 15px; box-shadow: 0 5px 20px rgba(0,0,0,0.1); }
        .card-header { background: linear-gradient(135deg, #667eea 0%, #764ba2 100%); color: #fff; border-radius: 15px 15px 0 0 !important; }
        .video-container {
            position: relative;
            width: 100%;
            border-radius: 10px;
            overflow: hidden;
            background: #000;
            min-height: 200px;
            display: flex;
            align-items: center;
            justify-content: center;
        }
        .video-placeholder {
            color: #fff;
            text-align: center;
            padding: 20px;
        }
        .video-placeholder i {
            font-size: 3rem;
            margin-bottom: 10px;
            opacity: 0.6;
        }
        #video { width: 100%; height: auto; max-height: 400px; object-fit: cover; display: none; }
        .btn-success { background: #28a745; border: none; }
        .btn-success:hover { transform: translateY(-2px); }
        .btn-success:disabled { opacity: 0.5; cursor: not-allowed; }
    </style>
</head>
<body>
    <nav class="navbar navbar-expand-lg">
        <div class="container-fluid">
            <a class="navbar-brand" href="#"><i class="fas fa-graduation-cap"></i> Attendance</a>
            <div class="d-flex">
                <a href="{% url 'lecturer_dashboard' %}" class="nav-link"><i class="fas fa-arrow-left"></i> Dashboard</a>
                <a href="{% url 'logout' %}" class="nav-link"><i class="fas fa-sign-out-alt"></i> Logout</a>
            </div>
        </div>
    </nav>

    <div class="container">
        <h2 class="mb-4"><i class="fas fa-user-check"></i> Mark Your Attendance</h2>

        <div class="row">
            <div class="col-md-6">
                <div class="card">
                    <div class="card-header"><h5><i class="fas fa-camera"></i> Face Capture</h5></div>
                    <div class="card-body">
                        <div class="video-container" id="videoContainer">
                            <div class="video-placeholder" id="videoPlaceholder">
                                <i class="fas fa-video-slash"></i>
                                <p>Waiting for location verification...</p>
                            </div>
                            <video id="video" autoplay playsinline></video>
                            <canvas id="canvas" style="display:none;"></canvas>
                        </div>
                        <button class="btn btn-success w-100 mt-3" id="captureBtn" disabled>
                            <i class="fas fa-camera"></i> Capture & Mark Attendance
                        </button>
                        <div id="status" class="mt-3"></div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
            <div class="col-md-6">
                <div class="card">
                    <div class="card-header"><h5><i class="fas fa-info-circle"></i> Instructions</h5></div>
                    <div class="card-body">
                        <ul>
                            <li>Ensure you are at the designated location.</li>
                            <li>Allow camera and location access.</li>
                            <li>Face should be clearly visible.</li>
                            <li>Click "Capture & Mark Attendance".</li>
                            <li>Location and face will be verified in real-time.</li>
                        </ul>
                        <div id="locationStatus" class="alert alert-secondary">Checking location...</div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>

    <script>
        const wsProtocol = window.location.protocol === 'https:' ? 'wss:' : 'ws:';
        const wsUrl = `${wsProtocol}//${window.location.host}/ws/lecturer-attendance/`;
        let ws = null;
        let stream = null;
        let locationVerified = false;
        let cameraReady = false;
        let processing = false;
        const video = document.getElementById('video');
        const canvas = document.getElementById('canvas');
        const statusDiv = document.getElementById('status');
        const locationStatus = document.getElementById('locationStatus');
        const captureBtn = document.getElementById('captureBtn');
        const videoPlaceholder = document.getElementById('videoPlaceholder');

        function connectWebSocket() {
            ws = new WebSocket(wsUrl);
            ws.onopen = function() {
                console.log('WebSocket connected');
                checkLocation();
            };
            ws.onmessage = function(e) {
                const data = JSON.parse(e.data);
                if (data.type === 'location_status') {
                    if (data.verified) {
                        locationVerified = true;
                        locationStatus.className = 'alert alert-success';
                        locationStatus.innerHTML = `<i class="fas fa-check-circle"></i> Location verified (${data.distance} meters)`;
                        // Start camera now that location is verified
                        if (!cameraReady) {
                            initCamera();
                        }
                    } else {
                        locationVerified = false;
                        locationStatus.className = 'alert alert-danger';
                        locationStatus.innerHTML = `<i class="fas fa-times-circle"></i> ${data.message}`;
                        captureBtn.disabled = true;
                        // Show placeholder, hide video if camera was active
                        if (cameraReady) {
                            stopCamera();
                        }
                    }
                } else if (data.type === 'attendance_result') {
                    processing = false;
                    captureBtn.disabled = false;
                    if (data.success) {
                        statusDiv.innerHTML = `<div class="alert alert-success"><i class="fas fa-check-circle"></i> ${data.message}</div>`;
                        captureBtn.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-check"></i> Done!';
                        captureBtn.disabled = true;
                        setTimeout(() => {
                            window.location.href = "{% url 'lecturer_dashboard' %}";
                        }, 2000);
                    } else {
                        statusDiv.innerHTML = `<div class="alert alert-danger"><i class="fas fa-exclamation-circle"></i> ${data.message}</div>`;
                        captureBtn.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-camera"></i> Capture & Mark Attendance';
                    }
                }
            };
            ws.onerror = function(err) {
                console.error('WebSocket error:', err);
                locationStatus.className = 'alert alert-danger';
                locationStatus.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-exclamation-triangle"></i> Connection error. Please refresh.';
            };
            ws.onclose = function() {
                console.log('WebSocket closed');
            };
        }

        function checkLocation() {
            if (!navigator.geolocation) {
                locationStatus.className = 'alert alert-danger';
                locationStatus.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-exclamation-triangle"></i> Geolocation not supported.';
                return;
            }
            navigator.geolocation.getCurrentPosition(
                (pos) => {
                    const lat = pos.coords.latitude;
                    const lon = pos.coords.longitude;
                    ws.send(JSON.stringify({
                        action: 'check_location',
                        latitude: lat,
                        longitude: lon
                    }));
                },
                (err) => {
                    locationStatus.className = 'alert alert-danger';
                    locationStatus.innerHTML = `<i class="fas fa-exclamation-triangle"></i> Error: ${err.message}`;
                }
            );
        }

        function initCamera() {
            if (cameraReady) return;
            videoPlaceholder.style.display = 'none';
            video.style.display = 'block';
            navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({ video: { facingMode: "user" } })
                .then(function(streamObj) {
                    stream = streamObj;
                    video.srcObject = stream;
                    cameraReady = true;
                    captureBtn.disabled = false;
                    statusDiv.innerHTML = '<div class="alert alert-success"><i class="fas fa-check"></i> Camera ready.</div>';
                })
                .catch(function(err) {
                    statusDiv.innerHTML = `<div class="alert alert-danger">Camera error: ${err.message}</div>`;
                    video.style.display = 'none';
                    videoPlaceholder.style.display = 'block';
                    videoPlaceholder.innerHTML = `<i class="fas fa-exclamation-triangle"></i><p>Camera access denied.</p>`;
                    captureBtn.disabled = true;
                });
        }

        function stopCamera() {
            if (stream) {
                stream.getTracks().forEach(t => t.stop());
                stream = null;
            }
            cameraReady = false;
            video.style.display = 'none';
            videoPlaceholder.style.display = 'block';
            videoPlaceholder.innerHTML = `<i class="fas fa-video-slash"></i><p>Location not verified. Please move to the designated area.</p>`;
            captureBtn.disabled = true;
        }

        function captureFace() {
            if (!locationVerified) {
                alert('Location not verified. Please check your location.');
                return;
            }
            if (!cameraReady) {
                alert('Camera not ready.');
                return;
            }
            if (processing) return;
            processing = true;
            captureBtn.disabled = true;
            captureBtn.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-spinner fa-spin"></i> Processing...';
            statusDiv.innerHTML = '<div class="alert alert-info">Processing...</div>';

            const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
            canvas.width = video.videoWidth;
            canvas.height = video.videoHeight;
            ctx.drawImage(video, 0, 0);
            const frameData = canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.8);

            ws.send(JSON.stringify({
                action: 'mark_attendance',
                frame: frameData
            }));
        }

        // Initialize
        window.onload = function() {
            connectWebSocket();
            // Camera will be started when location is verified
        };

        window.onbeforeunload = function() {
            if (ws) ws.close();
            if (stream) stream.getTracks().forEach(t => t.stop());
        };

        // Attach click listener to the button
        document.getElementById('captureBtn').addEventListener('click', captureFace);
    </script>
    <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/bootstrap@5.1.3/dist/js/bootstrap.bundle.min.js"></script>
</body>
</html>

                        </select>
                    </div>
                    <div class="col-md-2 d-flex align-items-end">
                        <div class="filter-actions">
                            <!-- Clear button only – resets all filters -->
                            <button type="button" class="btn btn-clear" id="clearBtn">
                                <i class="fas fa-undo-alt me-1"></i> Clear
                            </button>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </form>

                <div class="table-responsive">
                    <table class="table">
                        <thead>
                            <tr>
                                <th>Name</th>
                                <th>Username</th>
                                <th>Role</th>
                                <th>Assigned Course</th>

                            </tr>

                            {% for person in staff %}

                                <td><strong>{{ person.get_full_name }}</strong></td>
                                <td>{{ person.username }}</td>
                                <td><span class="badge">{{ person.get_user_type_display }}</span></td>

    <script>
        (function() {
            const form = document.getElementById('filterForm');
            const searchInput = document.getElementById('search');
            const roleSelect = document.getElementById('role');
            const courseSelect = document.getElementById('course');
            const clearBtn = document.getElementById('clearBtn');

            // Debounce helper for search input
            let debounceTimer = null;
            function submitForm() {
                form.submit();
            }

            // Auto-submit on select change
            roleSelect.addEventListener('change', submitForm);
            courseSelect.addEventListener('change', submitForm);

            // Auto-submit on search with debounce (300ms)
            searchInput.addEventListener('input', function() {
                clearTimeout(debounceTimer);
                debounceTimer = setTimeout(submitForm, 300);
            });

            // Clear button: reset all fields and submit
            clearBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
                searchInput.value = '';
                roleSelect.value = '';
                courseSelect.value = '';
                // Submit immediately (no debounce)
                form.submit();
            });

            // Also submit if user presses Enter in search field (optional)
            searchInput.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {
                if (e.key === 'Enter') {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    clearTimeout(debounceTimer);
                    submitForm();
                }
            });
        })();
    </script>

    <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/bootstrap@5.1.3/dist/js/bootstrap.bundle.min.js"></script>
</body>
</html>

                                <td>{% if record.location_verified %}<span class="badge bg-success"><i class="fas fa-check"></i></span>{% else %}<span class="badge bg-danger"><i class="fas fa-times"></i></span>{% endif %}</td>

                            <tr id="noRecords"><td colspan="7" class="text-center py-4"><i class="fas fa-inbox fa-3x text-muted mb-3"></i><p class="text-muted">No records yet</p></td></tr>
                            {% endfor %}
                        </tbody>
                    </table>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>

    <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/bootstrap@5.1.3/dist/js/bootstrap.bundle.min.js"></script>
    <script>
        let ws;
        let presentCount = {{ present_count }};
        let absentCount = {{ absent_count }};
        const enrolledCount = {{ enrolled_count }};
        function connectWebSocket() {
            const protocol = window.location.protocol === 'https:' ? 'wss:' : 'ws:';
            ws = new WebSocket(`${protocol}//${window.location.host}/ws/attendance/{{ session.id }}/`);
            ws.onopen = function() { console.log('WebSocket connected'); };
            ws.onmessage = function(e) {
                const data = JSON.parse(e.data);
                if (data.type === 'attendance_update') addAttendanceRow(data.data);
                else if (data.type === 'session_ended') handleSessionEnded();
            };
            ws.onerror = function(error) { console.error('WebSocket error:', error); };
            ws.onclose = function() { console.log('WebSocket disconnected'); };
        }
        function addAttendanceRow(data) {
            const noRecords = document.getElementById('noRecords');
            if (noRecords) noRecords.remove();
            const tbody = document.getElementById('attendanceTable').getElementsByTagName('tbody')[0];
            const newRow = tbody.insertRow(0);
            newRow.className = 'attendance-row';
            presentCount++;
            const rowCount = tbody.getElementsByTagName('tr').length;
            newRow.innerHTML = `
                <td>${rowCount}</td>
                <td>${data.student_name}</td>
                <td><span class="badge bg-primary">${data.roll_no}</span></td>
                <td>${data.timestamp}</td>
                <td><span class="badge bg-success"><i class="fas fa-check"></i></span></td>
                <td><span class="badge bg-success"><i class="fas fa-check"></i></span></td>
                <td><span class="badge bg-success">${data.status}</span></td>
            `;
            updateStats();
            presentCount++;
            absentCount = enrolledCount - presentCount; // recalc
            updateStats();
        }
        function updateStats() {
            document.getElementById('presentCount').textContent = presentCount;
            const absent = enrolledCount - presentCount;
            document.getElementById('absentCount').textContent = absent >= 0 ? absent : 0;
            const percentage = enrolledCount > 0 ? Math.round((presentCount / enrolledCount) * 100) : 0;
            document.getElementById('percentageCount').textContent = percentage + '%';
            // update total records if needed
            document.getElementById('recordCount').textContent = presentCount + absent;
        }
        function endSession() {
            if (confirm('End this session?')) {
                ws.send(JSON.stringify({ action: 'end_session' }));
                window.location.href = "{% url 'end_session' session.id %}";
            }
        }
        function handleSessionEnded() {
            document.getElementById('sessionStatusBadge').className = 'badge bg-secondary';
            document.getElementById('sessionStatusBadge').innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-stop-circle"></i> Ended';
            const endBtn = document.querySelector('.btn-danger');
            if (endBtn) { endBtn.disabled = true; endBtn.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-check"></i> Ended'; }
        }
        {% if session.is_active %} connectWebSocket(); {% endif %}
        window.onbeforeunload = function() { if (ws) ws.close(); };
    </script>
</body>
</html>

<!-- templates/attendance/staff_mark_attendance.html -->
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
    <title>Mark Attendance - Staff</title>
    <link href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/bootstrap@5.1.3/dist/css/bootstrap.min.css" rel="stylesheet">
    <link href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/font-awesome/6.0.0/css/all.min.css" rel="stylesheet">
    <style>
        /* Same styles as lecturer_mark_attendance.html, but change any lecturer-specific text */
        body { background: #f5f5f5; padding: 20px; }
        .navbar { background: linear-gradient(135deg, #2c3e50 0%, #34495e 100%); border-radius: 12px; margin-bottom: 30px; }
        .navbar-brand, .nav-link { color: #fff !important; }
        .card { border-radius: 15px; box-shadow: 0 5px 20px rgba(0,0,0,0.1); }
        .card-header { background: linear-gradient(135deg, #2c3e50 0%, #34495e 100%); color: #fff; border-radius: 15px 15px 0 0 !important; }
        .video-container {
            position: relative;
            width: 100%;
            border-radius: 10px;
            overflow: hidden;
            background: #000;
            min-height: 200px;
            display: flex;
            align-items: center;
            justify-content: center;
        }
        .video-placeholder { color: #fff; text-align: center; padding: 20px; }
        .video-placeholder i { font-size: 3rem; margin-bottom: 10px; opacity: 0.6; }
        #video { width: 100%; height: auto; max-height: 400px; object-fit: cover; display: none; }
        .btn-success { background: #28a745; border: none; }
        .btn-success:hover { transform: translateY(-2px); }
        .btn-success:disabled { opacity: 0.5; cursor: not-allowed; }
    </style>
</head>
<body>
    <nav class="navbar navbar-expand-lg">
        <div class="container-fluid">
            <a class="navbar-brand" href="{% url 'staff_dashboard' %}"><i class="fas fa-arrow-left"></i> Staff Dashboard</a>
            <div class="d-flex">
                <a href="{% url 'logout' %}" class="nav-link"><i class="fas fa-sign-out-alt"></i> Logout</a>
            </div>
        </div>
    </nav>

    <div class="container">
        <h2 class="mb-4"><i class="fas fa-user-check"></i> Mark Your Attendance</h2>

        <div class="row">
            <div class="col-md-6">
                <div class="card">
                    <div class="card-header"><h5><i class="fas fa-camera"></i> Face Capture</h5></div>
                    <div class="card-body">
                        <div class="video-container" id="videoContainer">
                            <div class="video-placeholder" id="videoPlaceholder">
                                <i class="fas fa-video-slash"></i>
                                <p>Waiting for location verification...</p>
                            </div>
                            <video id="video" autoplay playsinline></video>
                            <canvas id="canvas" style="display:none;"></canvas>
                        </div>
                        <button class="btn btn-success w-100 mt-3" id="captureBtn" disabled>
                            <i class="fas fa-camera"></i> Capture & Mark Attendance
                        </button>
                        <div id="status" class="mt-3"></div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
            <div class="col-md-6">
                <div class="card">
                    <div class="card-header"><h5><i class="fas fa-info-circle"></i> Instructions</h5></div>
                    <div class="card-body">
                        <ul>
                            <li>Ensure you are at the designated location.</li>
                            <li>Allow camera and location access.</li>
                            <li>Face should be clearly visible.</li>
                            <li>Click "Capture & Mark Attendance".</li>
                            <li>Location and face will be verified in real-time.</li>
                        </ul>
                        <div id="locationStatus" class="alert alert-secondary">Checking location...</div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>

    <script>
        const wsProtocol = window.location.protocol === 'https:' ? 'wss:' : 'ws:';
        const wsUrl = `${wsProtocol}//${window.location.host}/ws/staff-attendance/`;
        let ws = null;
        let stream = null;
        let locationVerified = false;
        let cameraReady = false;
        let processing = false;
        const video = document.getElementById('video');
        const canvas = document.getElementById('canvas');
        const statusDiv = document.getElementById('status');
        const locationStatus = document.getElementById('locationStatus');
        const captureBtn = document.getElementById('captureBtn');
        const videoPlaceholder = document.getElementById('videoPlaceholder');

        function connectWebSocket() {
            ws = new WebSocket(wsUrl);
            ws.onopen = function() {
                console.log('WebSocket connected');
                checkLocation();
            };
            ws.onmessage = function(e) {
                const data = JSON.parse(e.data);
                if (data.type === 'location_status') {
                    if (data.verified) {
                        locationVerified = true;
                        locationStatus.className = 'alert alert-success';
                        locationStatus.innerHTML = `<i class="fas fa-check-circle"></i> Location verified (${data.distance} meters)`;
                        if (!cameraReady) initCamera();
                    } else {
                        locationVerified = false;
                        locationStatus.className = 'alert alert-danger';
                        locationStatus.innerHTML = `<i class="fas fa-times-circle"></i> ${data.message}`;
                        captureBtn.disabled = true;
                        if (cameraReady) stopCamera();
                    }
                } else if (data.type === 'attendance_result') {
                    processing = false;
                    captureBtn.disabled = false;
                    if (data.success) {
                        statusDiv.innerHTML = `<div class="alert alert-success"><i class="fas fa-check-circle"></i> ${data.message}</div>`;
                        captureBtn.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-check"></i> Done!';
                        captureBtn.disabled = true;
                        setTimeout(() => {
                            window.location.href = "{% url 'staff_dashboard' %}";
                        }, 2000);
                    } else {
                        statusDiv.innerHTML = `<div class="alert alert-danger"><i class="fas fa-exclamation-circle"></i> ${data.message}</div>`;
                        captureBtn.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-camera"></i> Capture & Mark Attendance';
                    }
                }
            };
            ws.onerror = function(err) {
                console.error('WebSocket error:', err);
                locationStatus.className = 'alert alert-danger';
                locationStatus.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-exclamation-triangle"></i> Connection error. Please refresh.';
            };
            ws.onclose = function() { console.log('WebSocket closed'); };
        }

        function checkLocation() {
            if (!navigator.geolocation) {
                locationStatus.className = 'alert alert-danger';
                locationStatus.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-exclamation-triangle"></i> Geolocation not supported.';
                return;
            }
            navigator.geolocation.getCurrentPosition(
                (pos) => {
                    ws.send(JSON.stringify({
                        action: 'check_location',
                        latitude: pos.coords.latitude,
                        longitude: pos.coords.longitude
                    }));
                },
                (err) => {
                    locationStatus.className = 'alert alert-danger';
                    locationStatus.innerHTML = `<i class="fas fa-exclamation-triangle"></i> Error: ${err.message}`;
                }
            );
        }

        function initCamera() {
            if (cameraReady) return;
            videoPlaceholder.style.display = 'none';
            video.style.display = 'block';
            navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({ video: { facingMode: "user" } })
                .then((streamObj) => {
                    stream = streamObj;
                    video.srcObject = stream;
                    cameraReady = true;
                    captureBtn.disabled = false;
                    statusDiv.innerHTML = '<div class="alert alert-success"><i class="fas fa-check"></i> Camera ready.</div>';
                })
                .catch((err) => {
                    statusDiv.innerHTML = `<div class="alert alert-danger">Camera error: ${err.message}</div>`;
                    video.style.display = 'none';
                    videoPlaceholder.style.display = 'block';
                    videoPlaceholder.innerHTML = `<i class="fas fa-exclamation-triangle"></i><p>Camera access denied.</p>`;
                    captureBtn.disabled = true;
                });
        }

        function stopCamera() {
            if (stream) { stream.getTracks().forEach(t => t.stop()); stream = null; }
            cameraReady = false;
            video.style.display = 'none';
            videoPlaceholder.style.display = 'block';
            videoPlaceholder.innerHTML = `<i class="fas fa-video-slash"></i><p>Location not verified. Please move to the designated area.</p>`;
            captureBtn.disabled = true;
        }

        function captureFace() {
            if (!locationVerified) { alert('Location not verified.'); return; }
            if (!cameraReady) { alert('Camera not ready.'); return; }
            if (processing) return;
            processing = true;
            captureBtn.disabled = true;
            captureBtn.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-spinner fa-spin"></i> Processing...';
            statusDiv.innerHTML = '<div class="alert alert-info">Processing...</div>';

            const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
            canvas.width = video.videoWidth;
            canvas.height = video.videoHeight;
            ctx.drawImage(video, 0, 0);
            const frameData = canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.8);

            ws.send(JSON.stringify({ action: 'mark_attendance', frame: frameData }));
        }

        window.onload = function() { connectWebSocket(); };
        window.onbeforeunload = function() { if (ws) ws.close(); if (stream) stream.getTracks().forEach(t => t.stop()); };
        document.getElementById('captureBtn').addEventListener('click', captureFace);
    </script>
    <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/bootstrap@5.1.3/dist/js/bootstrap.bundle.min.js"></script>
</body>
</html>

    <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/bootstrap@5.1.3/dist/js/bootstrap.bundle.min.js"></script>
    <script>
        document.getElementById('course').addEventListener('change', loadSectionsAndSubjects);
        document.getElementById('semester').addEventListener('change', loadSectionsAndSubjects);

        function loadSectionsAndSubjects() {
            const courseId = document.getElementById('course').value;
            const semester = document.getElementById('semester').value;
            if (courseId && semester) {
                fetch(`/api/sections/${courseId}/${semester}/`)
                    .then(r => r.json())
                    .then(data => {
                        const sel = document.getElementById('section');
                        sel.innerHTML = '<option value="">Select Section</option>';
                        data.forEach(s => { sel.innerHTML += `<option value="${s.id}">${s.name}</option>`; });
                        sel.disabled = false;
                    });
                fetch(`/api/subjects/${courseId}/${semester}/`)
                    .then(r => r.json())
                    .then(data => {
                        const sel = document.getElementById('subject');
                        sel.innerHTML = '<option value="">Select Subject</option>';
                        data.forEach(s => { sel.innerHTML += `<option value="${s.id}">${s.code} - ${s.name}</option>`; });
                        sel.disabled = false;
                    });
            } else {
                document.getElementById('section').disabled = true;
                document.getElementById('section').innerHTML = '<option value="">Select Course & Semester First</option>';
                document.getElementById('subject').disabled = true;
                document.getElementById('subject').innerHTML = '<option value="">Select Course & Semester First</option>';
            }
        }

        function getCurrentLocation() {
            if (!navigator.geolocation) { alert('Geolocation not supported'); return; }
            const status = document.getElementById('locationStatus');
            status.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-spinner fa-spin"></i> Getting location...';
            navigator.geolocation.getCurrentPosition(
                (pos) => {
                    const lat = pos.coords.latitude, lon = pos.coords.longitude;
                    document.getElementById('latitude').value = lat;
                    document.getElementById('longitude').value = lon;
                    document.getElementById('startBtn').disabled = false;
                    const mapDiv = document.getElementById('locationMap');
                    mapDiv.style.display = 'block';
                    mapDiv.innerHTML = `<iframe width="100%" height="200" frameborder="0" scrolling="no" marginheight="0" marginwidth="0" src="https://maps.google.com/maps?q=${lat},${lon}&z=17&output=embed"></iframe>`;
                    status.innerHTML = `<span class="text-success"><i class="fas fa-check-circle"></i> Location captured: ${lat.toFixed(6)}, ${lon.toFixed(6)}</span>`;
                },
                (err) => {
                    let msg = 'Error: ';
                    switch(err.code) {
                        case err.PERMISSION_DENIED: msg += 'Permission denied'; break;
                        case err.POSITION_UNAVAILABLE: msg += 'Unavailable'; break;
                        case err.TIMEOUT: msg += 'Timeout'; break;
                        default: msg += err.message;
                    }
                    status.innerHTML = `<span class="text-danger"><i class="fas fa-exclamation-circle"></i> ${msg}</span>`;
                },
                { enableHighAccuracy: true, timeout: 10000 }
            );
        }
    </script>
</body>
</html>

    <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/bootstrap@5.1.3/dist/js/bootstrap.bundle.min.js"></script>
    <script>
        let ws = null, stream = null, isLocationVerified = false, isCameraReady = false, studentLocation = null;
        const studentId = {{ student.id }}, sessionId = {{ session.id }}, sessionLat = {{ session.latitude }}, sessionLon = {{ session.longitude }}, sessionRadius = {{ session.radius_feet }};

        function connectWebSocket() {
            const protocol = window.location.protocol === 'https:' ? 'wss:' : 'ws:';
            ws = new WebSocket(`${protocol}//${window.location.host}/ws/attendance/${sessionId}/`);
            ws.onopen = function() { ws.send(JSON.stringify({ action: 'init_student', student_id: studentId })); };
            ws.onmessage = function(e) {
                const data = JSON.parse(e.data);
                if (data.type === 'attendance_result') handleAttendanceResult(data);
                else if (data.type === 'session_ended') handleSessionEnded();
                else if (data.type === 'error') showMessage(data.message, 'error');
            };
            ws.onerror = function() { showMessage('Connection error.', 'error'); };
        }

        function checkLocation() {
            const btn = document.getElementById('checkLocationBtn');
            const alert = document.getElementById('locationAlert');
            btn.disabled = true; btn.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-spinner fa-spin"></i> Checking...';
            alert.className = 'alert alert-info'; alert.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-spinner fa-spin"></i> Getting location...';
            updateStepStatus(1, 'active');
            updateLocationStatus('Checking...', 'checking');
            if (!navigator.geolocation) { handleLocationError('Geolocation not supported'); return; }
            navigator.geolocation.getCurrentPosition(
                (pos) => {
                    const lat = pos.coords.latitude, lon = pos.coords.longitude;
                    studentLocation = { latitude: lat, longitude: lon };
                    const distance = calculateDistance(sessionLat, sessionLon, lat, lon);
                    document.getElementById('distanceInfo').style.display = 'flex';
                    document.getElementById('distanceText').textContent = distance.toFixed(2) + ' feet';
                    if (distance <= sessionRadius) { handleLocationSuccess(distance); }
                    else { handleLocationOutOfRange(distance); }
                },
                (err) => {
                    let msg = 'Error: ';
                    switch(err.code) {
                        case err.PERMISSION_DENIED: msg += 'Permission denied'; break;
                        case err.POSITION_UNAVAILABLE: msg += 'Unavailable'; break;
                        case err.TIMEOUT: msg += 'Timeout'; break;
                        default: msg += err.message;
                    }
                    handleLocationError(msg);
                },
                { enableHighAccuracy: true, timeout: 15000 }
            );
        }

        function handleLocationSuccess(distance) {
            isLocationVerified = true;
            updateLocationStatus(`✅ Verified (${distance.toFixed(1)} ft)`, 'success');
            updateStepStatus(1, 'completed');
            document.getElementById('locationAlert').className = 'alert alert-success';
            document.getElementById('locationAlert').innerHTML = `<i class="fas fa-check-circle"></i> Location verified (${distance.toFixed(1)} ft)`;
            const btn = document.getElementById('checkLocationBtn');
            btn.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-check-circle"></i> Verified ✓';
            btn.className = 'btn btn-success btn-lg w-100 mb-3';
            btn.disabled = true;
            document.getElementById('cameraSection').style.display = 'block';
            document.getElementById('videoOverlay').style.display = 'none';
            initCamera();
            updateCameraStatus('Initializing...', 'checking');
            updateStepStatus(2, 'active');
            document.getElementById('markAttendanceBtn').disabled = false;
            document.getElementById('buttonHint').innerHTML = '✅ Face visible, click Mark Attendance';
        }

        function handleLocationOutOfRange(distance) {
            isLocationVerified = false;
            updateLocationStatus(`❌ Out of range (${distance.toFixed(1)} ft / ${sessionRadius} ft)`, 'error');
            updateStepStatus(1, 'error');
            document.getElementById('locationAlert').className = 'alert alert-danger';
            document.getElementById('locationAlert').innerHTML = `<i class="fas fa-times-circle"></i> Outside range (${distance.toFixed(1)} ft)`;
            const btn = document.getElementById('checkLocationBtn');
            btn.disabled = false;
            btn.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-redo"></i> Retry';
            btn.className = 'btn btn-warning btn-lg w-100 mb-3';
            document.getElementById('cameraSection').style.display = 'none';
            document.getElementById('markAttendanceBtn').disabled = true;
            document.getElementById('buttonHint').innerHTML = '⚠️ Move closer and retry';
        }

        function handleLocationError(msg) {
            isLocationVerified = false;
            updateLocationStatus('❌ ' + msg, 'error');
            updateStepStatus(1, 'error');
            document.getElementById('locationAlert').className = 'alert alert-danger';
            document.getElementById('locationAlert').innerHTML = `<i class="fas fa-exclamation-triangle"></i> ${msg}`;
            const btn = document.getElementById('checkLocationBtn');
            btn.disabled = false;
            btn.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-redo"></i> Retry';
            btn.className = 'btn btn-warning btn-lg w-100 mb-3';
            document.getElementById('buttonHint').innerHTML = '⚠️ Location required';
        }

        async function initCamera() {
            try {
                stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({ video: { facingMode: "user", width: { ideal: 640 }, height: { ideal: 480 } } });
                const video = document.getElementById('video');
                video.srcObject = stream;
                video.onloadedmetadata = function() { isCameraReady = true; updateCameraStatus('✅ Ready', 'success'); };
            } catch (err) {
                updateCameraStatus('❌ Camera denied: ' + err.message, 'error');
                document.getElementById('markAttendanceBtn').disabled = true;
            }
        }

        async function markAttendance() {
            if (!isLocationVerified) { showMessage('Verify location first!', 'error'); return; }
            if (!isCameraReady) { showMessage('Camera not ready.', 'error'); return; }
            const btn = document.getElementById('markAttendanceBtn');
            btn.disabled = true; btn.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-spinner fa-spin"></i> Processing...';
            updateFaceStatus('Capturing...', 'checking');
            updateStepStatus(2, 'active');
            try {
                const video = document.getElementById('video');
                const canvas = document.getElementById('canvas');
                canvas.width = video.videoWidth; canvas.height = video.videoHeight;
                const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
                ctx.drawImage(video, 0, 0);
                const frameData = canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.8);
                ws.send(JSON.stringify({
                    action: 'mark_attendance',
                    frame: frameData,
                    latitude: studentLocation.latitude,
                    longitude: studentLocation.longitude
                }));
            } catch (err) {
                showMessage('Error: ' + err.message, 'error');
                resetMarkButton();
            }
        }

        function handleAttendanceResult(data) {
            if (data.success) {
                updateStepStatus(2, 'completed'); updateStepStatus(3, 'completed');
                updateFaceStatus('✅ Verified', 'success');
                updateAttendanceStatus('✅ Marked!', 'success');
                showMessage('🎉 ' + data.message, 'success');
                const btn = document.getElementById('markAttendanceBtn');
                btn.disabled = true; btn.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-check-circle"></i> Done!';
                btn.className = 'btn btn-success btn-lg w-100 mt-3';
                document.getElementById('buttonHint').innerHTML = '✅ Redirecting...';
                setTimeout(() => { window.location.href = "{% url 'student_dashboard' %}"; }, 2000);
            } else {
                updateFaceStatus('❌ ' + data.message, 'error');
                showMessage(data.message, 'error');
                resetMarkButton();
            }
        }

        function handleSessionEnded() {
            showMessage('Session ended by lecturer.', 'error');
            document.getElementById('markAttendanceBtn').disabled = true;
            document.getElementById('buttonHint').innerHTML = 'Session ended.';
            setTimeout(() => { window.location.href = "{% url 'student_dashboard' %}"; }, 2000);
        }

        function calculateDistance(lat1, lon1, lat2, lon2) {
            const R = 3958.8; const dLat = toRad(lat2-lat1), dLon = toRad(lon2-lon1);
            const a = Math.sin(dLat/2)**2 + Math.cos(toRad(lat1)) * Math.cos(toRad(lat2)) * Math.sin(dLon/2)**2;
            const c = 2 * Math.atan2(Math.sqrt(a), Math.sqrt(1-a));
            return R * c * 5280;
        }
        function toRad(deg) { return deg * (Math.PI/180); }

        function updateStepStatus(step, status) {
            const el = document.getElementById('step' + step);
            el.classList.remove('active', 'completed');
            if (status === 'active') el.classList.add('active');
            else if (status === 'completed') el.classList.add('completed');
            else if (status === 'error') el.querySelector('.step-number').style.background = '#dc3545';
        }
        function updateLocationStatus(text, type) {
            const box = document.getElementById('locationStatusBox');
            const txt = document.getElementById('locationStatusText');
            box.className = 'status-box';
            txt.textContent = text;
            if (type === 'success') box.classList.add('status-success');
            else if (type === 'error') box.classList.add('status-error');
            else if (type === 'checking') box.classList.add('status-checking');
        }
        function updateCameraStatus(text, type) {
            const box = document.getElementById('cameraStatusBox');
            const txt = document.getElementById('cameraStatusText');
            box.className = 'status-box';
            txt.textContent = text;
            if (type === 'success') box.classList.add('status-success');
            else if (type === 'error') box.classList.add('status-error');
            else if (type === 'checking') box.classList.add('status-checking');
        }
        function updateFaceStatus(text, type) {
            const box = document.getElementById('faceStatusBox');
            const txt = document.getElementById('faceStatusText');
            box.className = 'status-box';
            txt.textContent = text;
            if (type === 'success') box.classList.add('status-success');
            else if (type === 'error') box.classList.add('status-error');
            else if (type === 'checking') box.classList.add('status-checking');
        }
        function updateAttendanceStatus(text, type) {
            const box = document.getElementById('attendanceStatusBox');
            const txt = document.getElementById('attendanceStatusText');
            box.className = 'status-box';
            txt.textContent = text;
            if (type === 'success') box.classList.add('status-success');
            else if (type === 'error') box.classList.add('status-error');
        }
        function showMessage(msg, type) {
            const div = document.getElementById('resultMessage');
            div.innerHTML = `<div class="alert alert-${type === 'success' ? 'success' : 'danger'} mt-3"><i class="fas fa-${type === 'success' ? 'check-circle' : 'times-circle'}"></i> ${msg}</div>`;
        }
        function resetMarkButton() {
            const btn = document.getElementById('markAttendanceBtn');
            btn.disabled = false; btn.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-user-check"></i> Mark Attendance';
        }

        window.onload = function() {
            if ({{ session.is_active|lower }} && {{ is_enrolled|lower }} && !{{ already_marked|lower }}) connectWebSocket();
        };
        window.onbeforeunload = function() {
            if (stream) stream.getTracks().forEach(t => t.stop());
            if (ws) ws.close();
        };
    </script>
</body>
</html>
